% Custom head/foot rule pairs (thick primaryBlue + thin lightBlue), overlay via [C]
\newcommand{\customheadrule}{%
    \begin{tikzpicture}[remember picture, overlay]
        \draw[primaryBlue, line width=1.2pt]
            ([yshift=-0.5cm, xshift=2cm]current page.north west) --
            ([yshift=-0.5cm, xshift=-2cm]current page.north east);
        \draw[lightBlue, line width=0.4pt]
            ([yshift=-0.6cm, xshift=2cm]current page.north west) --
            ([yshift=-0.6cm, xshift=-2cm]current page.north east);
    \end{tikzpicture}%
}
\newcommand{\customfootrule}{%
    \begin{tikzpicture}[remember picture, overlay]
        \draw[lightBlue, line width=0.4pt]
            ([yshift=1.55cm, xshift=2cm]current page.south west) --
            ([yshift=1.55cm, xshift=-2cm]current page.south east);
        \draw[primaryBlue, line width=1.2pt]
            ([yshift=1.45cm, xshift=2cm]current page.south west) --
            ([yshift=1.45cm, xshift=-2cm]current page.south east);
    \end{tikzpicture}%
}
\fancyhead[C]{\customheadrule}
\fancyfoot[C]{\customfootrule}

\newtcolorbox{topicbox}[1]{
    enhanced, colback=primaryBlue!8, colframe=primaryBlue,
    coltitle=white, fonttitle=\bfseries\Large,
    title={#1},
    attach boxed title to top center={yshift=-3mm},
    minipage boxed title*=-15mm,
    boxed title style={colback=primaryBlue, rounded corners},
    rounded corners, arc=4mm, boxrule=1.8pt,
    breakable,
    drop shadow={shadow xshift=2pt, shadow yshift=-2pt, opacity=0.3},
    before skip=15pt, after skip=10pt
}

\newtcolorbox{groupbox}[1]{
    enhanced, colback=secondaryBlue, colframe=accentBlue,
    coltext=white, boxrule=0.8pt, arc=1.5mm,
    left=8pt, right=8pt, top=4pt, bottom=4pt,
    before skip=12pt, after skip=8pt
}

% ============================================================
% COMMANDS
% ============================================================
\newcounter{qcounter}
\newcommand{\question}[2][]{%
    \stepcounter{qcounter}%
    \par\vspace{6pt}%
    \noindent\textcolor{primaryBlue}{\textbf{Câu \theqcounter.}}~%
    \textcolor{secondaryBlue}{\small\textbf{[P20 STUDY]}}~%
    \if\relax\detokenize{#1}\relax\else\textcolor{gray}{\small\textit{(#1)}}~\fi\\[1pt]
    \noindent #2%
}

\newcommand{\choiceitem}[2]{%
    \tikz[baseline=-0.5ex]{%
        \node[draw=primaryBlue, circle, minimum size=1.5em, inner sep=0pt, thick] {\textbf{#1}};%
    }~#2%
}
\newcommand{\choiceitemBox}[2]{%
    \tikz[baseline=-0.5ex]{%
        \node[draw=secondaryBlue, rectangle, minimum size=1.3em, inner sep=0pt, thick, rounded corners=1pt] {\textbf{#1}};%
    }~#2%
}

\newcounter{subquestioncounter}
\newcommand{\tfcircle}{%
    \raisebox{1.1pt}{\tikz[baseline=(tfcircleNode.center)]{
        \node[draw=primaryBlue, circle, minimum size=1.5em,
              inner sep=0pt, outer sep=0pt, line width=1pt] (tfcircleNode) {};%
    }}%
}
\newcommand{\truefalsehead}{%
    \par\vspace{6pt}%
    \setcounter{subquestioncounter}{0}%
    \begingroup
    \setstretch{1.0}%
    \renewcommand{\arraystretch}{1.4}%
    \small
    \begin{longtable}{|>{\raggedright\arraybackslash}m{0.72\textwidth}|%
                       >{\centering\arraybackslash}m{0.08\textwidth}|%
                       >{\centering\arraybackslash}m{0.08\textwidth}|}
    \hline
    \rowcolor{pastelBlue}
    \textbf{Mệnh đề} & \textbf{Đúng} & \textbf{Sai} \\
    \hline
    \endfirsthead
    \hline
    \rowcolor{pastelBlue}
    \textbf{Mệnh đề} & \textbf{Đúng} & \textbf{Sai} \\
    \hline
    \endhead
    \endfoot
    \endlastfoot
}
\newcommand{\truefalseitem}[1]{%
    \stepcounter{subquestioncounter}%
    (\alph{subquestioncounter})~#1 & \tfcircle & \tfcircle \\
    \hline
}
\newcommand{\truefalseend}{%
    \end{longtable}%
    \endgroup
}

\newcommand{\dropbox}[1][2cm]{%
    $\vcenter{\hbox{%
        \tikz{%
            \draw[borderGray, thick, dashed, rounded corners=3pt] (0,0) rectangle (#1, 0.6cm);%
            \fill[grayLight, rounded corners=2pt] (0.03,0.03) rectangle ({#1-0.03}, 0.57);%
        }%
    }}$%
}
\newenvironment{readingpassage}[1][]{%
    \begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced, colback=grayLight, colframe=secondaryBlue,
        coltitle=textDark, fonttitle=\bfseries, title={#1},
        rounded corners, arc=3mm, boxrule=1pt,
        left=15pt, right=15pt, top=10pt, bottom=10pt,
        breakable, before skip=10pt, after skip=10pt, drop fuzzy shadow]
    \setstretch{1.3}
}{\end{tcolorbox}}

\newcommand{\dragitems}[1]{
    \par\vspace{2pt}
    \begin{tcolorbox}[enhanced, colback=pastelBlue, colframe=secondaryBlue,
        rounded corners, arc=2mm, boxrule=1pt,
        title={\color{textDark}\small\textbf{Ngân hàng từ:}},
        fonttitle=\small, coltitle=textDark,
        attach boxed title to top left={yshift=-2mm, xshift=3mm},
        boxed title style={colback=pastelBlue, boxrule=0pt}]
    \centering
    #1
    \end{tcolorbox}\par\vspace{2pt}
}
\newcommand{\dragitem}[1]{
    \tcbox[enhanced, colback=white, colframe=primaryBlue,
        rounded corners, arc=2mm, boxrule=1pt,
        left=5pt, right=5pt, top=2pt, bottom=2pt,
        nobeforeafter, tcbox raise base,
        drop shadow={shadow xshift=1pt, shadow yshift=-1pt, opacity=0.2}]{#1}~
}

\newcommand{\partbanner}[2]{%
\begin{center}
\begin{tikzpicture}
    \node[fill=primaryBlue, rounded corners=12pt,
          inner sep=18pt, drop shadow={shadow xshift=3pt, shadow yshift=-3pt}] {
        \begin{minipage}{0.88\textwidth}
            \centering
            {\color{white}\fontsize{22}{28}\selectfont\textbf{#1}}\\[6pt]
            {\color{pastelBlue}\fontsize{16}{20}\selectfont\textbf{#2}}
        \end{minipage}
    };
\end{tikzpicture}
\end{center}
}

\newcommand{\subheading}[1]{%
    \par\vspace{10pt}%
    \noindent{\color{accentBlue}\Large\textbf{#1}}%
    \par\vspace{6pt}%
}

\subheading{1.2. Cấu trúc tổng quát}

\begin{itemize}[leftmargin=*, itemsep=5pt, label=\textcolor{primaryBlue}{\textbullet}]
\item \textbf{Tổng số câu hỏi:} 50 câu.
\item \textbf{Thời gian làm bài:} 60 phút.
\item \textbf{Dạng thức:} Trắc nghiệm khách quan (4 lựa chọn A, B, C, D).
\item \textbf{Cách tính điểm:} 1 điểm/câu đúng. Tổng điểm: 50 điểm.
\end{itemize}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{You know the party well.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Student 1: "Can you come with us in the weekend picnic?" Student 2: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Whether my parents will let me or not is the question.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The teacher is asking us to submit the paper next week.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The weather is so nice for a picnic.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Well, I thought it was yesterday.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Jenny: "Thanks for the nice gift you bought to us!" Peter: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{All right. Do you know how much it costs?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Not at all, don't mention it.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Actually speaking, I myself don't like it.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Welcome! it's very nice of you.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Kevin: "I don't know if I should join the new coding bootcamp. It looks difficult." Emma: "Come on, \underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} You’ll never know unless you try!"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{take it easy}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{give it a shot}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{make it worse}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{leave it behind}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{David: "I didn’t get the scholarship I applied for. I feel so disappointed." Emma: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{That’s unfortunate, but perhaps there’s a better opportunity waiting for you.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You should give up and stop applying for scholarships.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Well, at least you didn’t waste time applying for it.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{That’s great! You must be so happy about it.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Sophia: "Would it be possible for me to take next Friday off? I have a family event to attend." Manager: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I suppose so, but remember that frequent absences might affect your performance review.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You’ve already taken quite a few days off recently. Are you sure this is necessary?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{That depends. Will you be available to handle urgent matters remotely?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I see. If you can delegate your urgent tasks, I don’t see a problem with that.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Olivia: "We’re analyzing market trends for emerging technology. Could you share the key innovations your company is currently focusing on?" Daniel: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{That’s not really something I can discuss. You should check our website for general information.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Why are you asking? I don’t see how this relates to your research.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Certainly. Our main focus is on AI-driven automation, improving cybersecurity measures, and enhancing user experience through data analytics.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I’m not sure. We just develop whatever seems profitable at the moment.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{John: "I want to buy a dress for Daisy for her birthday. Who should get advice from?" Terry: "You should ask Jane. She \underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}."}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{has an eye for clothes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{knows what to do with dresses.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{knows her way.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{gives some good judgements.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{"How have you been recently?" - "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{It’s too late now}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Pretty busy, I think}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{By bus, I think}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{No, I will not be busy}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Sam is offering to bring William another cup of coffee. Sam: "Would you like another coffee?" William: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I’d love one}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Willingly}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Very kind of your part}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{It’s a pleasure}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Ash: "I'm thinking of going to the Hotdog Festival." Ben: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Yeah, once a year, only in summer.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You must be kidding. It's 120 dollars a day.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Not that expensive. But I don't prefer.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{What on Earth is that.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Mark: "Would you like to join us for a barbecue this weekend?" Anna: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{No, I don’t like eating.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{That sounds great! What time?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I prefer staying at home.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{It’s none of your business.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{David: "I’m thinking of applying for a scholarship to study abroad." Sarah: "That’s a great idea! \underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Break a leg!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Pull yourself together!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Go ahead!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{You’re welcome!}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Anna: "I’ve been working overtime every day this month, but my boss still doesn’t appreciate my effort." Ben: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Maybe you should discuss this with your boss and see what can be improved.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{That’s great! Hard work always pays off in the end.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{You should have known better than to expect appreciation.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I think quitting is the only option left for you.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Emily: "I can’t believe I forgot my wallet at home. This is so embarrassing!" Ryan: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{That’s a shame. You should be more careful next time.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Don’t worry. I’ll cover for you this time.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Well, that’s what happens when you’re absent-minded.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{You’d better go home and get it right now.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Anna: "I just got promoted at work!" Lisa: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{That’s a pity.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Congratulations! You deserve it.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I have no idea.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{You should quit now.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{David: "Could you help me carry this heavy box?" Jake: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I’d love to, but I hurt my back.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{No, do it yourself.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{That’s not my business.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I don’t care about it.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Sophie: "Do you mind if I take this seat?" Mark: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Yes, I’d rather you didn’t.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{No, be my guest.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Sorry, I have no seat.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Yes, it’s a great idea.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{James: "I was wondering if I could take part in your debate team. I believe I can bring in some great arguments." Sophia: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I appreciate your enthusiasm, but we have reached the maximum number of members.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You’re highly welcome, but I think you might struggle to keep up with us.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I’m afraid not. You don’t bring your own microphone today.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I don’t think you’re a good fit, so it’s better not to join us.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Alex: "Can you let me know what time the bus comes tomorrow?" Jamie: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{No problem! The bus usually arrives around 8:15 AM, so we should be ready by then.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You’ll have to check the schedule yourself; I’m not sure.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I don’t know why you’d want to take the bus. It’s usually late.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{It’s always a hassle waiting for buses anyway.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Emma: "Can you join the group presentation next week?" Alex: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{"Sure, I’ll join. What part would you like me to cover?"}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{"I’m busy next week, so I’m not sure I can help out."}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{"I suppose I can try, but I’m not sure I’ll do well."}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{"Oh, presentations are always a hassle for me."}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Jamie: "Hi, Taylor. I wanted to ask if I could take a day off next week. Is that possible?" Taylor: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Sure, just make sure to tie up loose ends before you go.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I’m afraid you might be pushing your luck with that request.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{No problem, but don’t leave me hanging while you’re gone.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I suppose you can take a day off, but I’ll have to think it over.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Pha and Tam are talking about sharing household chores. Pha: "From my point of view, all family members should share the chores equally." Tam: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. It is our responsibility in the family.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{It's a breathtaking view}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You lied to me}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{But you're right}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{There's no doubt about it}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Mr Nam and Mrs Lan are talking about retirement age. Mr Nam: “As far as I know, women should retire later than men.” Mrs Lan: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Physically, they are weaker and they need to retire earlier.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I'm sorry about that}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Not at all. Go ahead}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{You lied to me}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I don't think so}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Hellen and Mark are discussing taking morning exercise. Hellen: “I think taking morning exercise everyday is very good for our health.” Mark: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} It improves our strength, balance and flexibility.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{You’re exactly right}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{What nonsense}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Not so bad. And you?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Why not? Believe me!}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Baker and Barbara are talking each other about safe driving. Baker: “In my opinion, women often drive more carefully than men.” Barbara: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} They usually drive more slowly.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Never mind}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{What nonsense}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Absolutely}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Yes, please}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Hellen and Frank are talking about the environment. Hellen: "Everyone should be aware of the importance of a good environment." Frank: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. It provides us with clean water and fresh air.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I'm sorry about that}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Not at all. Go ahead}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Not so bad. And you?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{You can say that again}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Joana and David, two students, are talking about studying abroad. Joana: “I think studying abroad is the only way to get a well-paid job.” David: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. There’re still many different ways to get it.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{You’re exactly right}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I don’t think so}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{That’s what I think}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{There’s no doubt about it}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{An: "I think we should try that new restaurant downtown this weekend." Binh: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I don’t like trying new things.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{That sounds like a great idea!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Why do we need to go out?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I already have other plans.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Olivia: "How about grabbing dinner at that new restaurant tonight?" Ethan: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Guess what. Maybe if I can swing it tonight.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Are the dishes there expensive?}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Dining out is my cup of tea.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I’d be down for that!}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Student 1: "Are you joining us for the game tonight?" Student 2: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I hope we win this time!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Sorry. I have a test to study for.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The coach is really pushing us hard.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I’m not sure. I wanna stay alone.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Phuc: "Should we order pizza or sushi for dinner?" Loan: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I think pizza would be a crowd-pleaser tonight.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Sushi has more variety, so it’s hard to choose.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{While sushi is delicious, I’d love to eat spicy dishes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I enjoy both, but I’d rather keep it simple tonight.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Peter: "I thought your baseball game was a lot better, Phil." Phil: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{You’ve got to be kidding. I thought it was bad.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Yes, please. Just a little.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Thank you. I’d love to.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{No, thanks. I think I can do.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Jake: "I’ve been working so hard, but it feels like nothing is going right." Lily: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Honestly, it’s not that bad.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Yeah. I think you can do it.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Oh, I’m sorry.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Don’t worry, you’ve got this.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{David: "Are you planning to take the train or drive to the conference?" Sarah: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Neither. I’m going to fly.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{That’s impossible. I can’t afford a ticket.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Yes, I’d like a ride. Thank you.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Yes, I am.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Oliver: "Would you like to take responsibility for organizing the team meeting next week?" Sophia: "Definitely, \underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I’ll take a moment.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I’ll take charge.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I’ll take a seat.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I’ll take advice.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Sarah: "I can't believe the presentation went so badly. Do you think it was my fault?" John: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Honestly, it wasn’t entirely your fault, but there were some mistakes that could have been avoided.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{It's all your fault; you should have prepared better.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I think we both played a part in it, but it was mostly the team's responsibility.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{It wasn't your fault at all, it was mainly the technical issues.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Daisy: “What an attractive hair style you have got, Mary!” Mary: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Thank you very much. I’m afraid}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{You’re telling a lie}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Thank you for your compliment!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I don’t like your sayings}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Two students are discussing their study. Student 1: “I think the teacher should give us more exercises.” Student 2: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Doing exercises helps us revise a lot of knowledge we’ve learnt.”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Yes, let’s}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Ok}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{That’s rubbish}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{That’s what I am thinking}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Lan: "Happy birthday! This is a small present for you." Nga: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{What a pity!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{How terrible!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Have a good time!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{How beautiful it is! Thanks}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Jane: “Would you mind if I use your computer for an hour?” Tony: “\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}”}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Not at all. I’ve finished my job}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Yes, you can’t use it}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Of course. I still need it now}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Yes, it’s all right}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Trắc nghiệm]{Minh is talking to Thomas after his test. Minh: "My first English test was not as good as I expected." Thomas: "\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}"}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Good Heavens!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Never mind, better job next time!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{That's brilliant enough!}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{It's okay. Don't worry}\par\vspace{4pt}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{remain calm}\par\vspace{4pt}

\newpage
\begin{topicbox}{\large DẠNG 4: SẮP XẾP HỘI THOẠI}
\textit{Sắp xếp các câu cho sẵn thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh, sau đó chọn phương án đúng.}
\end{topicbox}
\setcounter{qcounter}{0}
\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{\alph*.}, leftmargin=2em, itemsep=3pt, topsep=2pt]
\item Right, but I assumed "today" meant after you finished your part, so I waited.
\item You promised to help me with the project today, remember?
\item No, I clearly remember you agreeing to today.
\item Wait, so you were waiting for me to start before you could help?
\item I did, but didn’t we say tomorrow? I thought you needed more time.
\item Exactly! I thought I was keeping my promise by giving you space to get ahead.
\end{enumerate}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{d -- a -- b -- e -- d -- f}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{b -- e -- c -- a -- d -- f}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{d -- f -- b -- a -- c -- e}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{b -- a -- c -- e -- d -- f}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{\alph*.}, leftmargin=2em, itemsep=3pt, topsep=2pt]
\item I think it’s at 3 PM, but didn’t they mention a delay earlier?
\item Let me double-check, just in case.
\item I thought it was on schedule.
\item Do you know what time the flight departs?
\end{enumerate}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\choiceitem{A}{d -- a -- c -- b}\hfill \choiceitem{B}{d -- b -- c -- a}\hfill \choiceitem{C}{d -- c -- b -- a}\hfill \choiceitem{D}{d -- c -- a -- b}}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{\alph*.}, leftmargin=2em, itemsep=3pt, topsep=2pt]
\item Technically, yes, but do you really want avocado pits in there for years?
\item Yes, but not everything - some things take too long to break down.
\item Do we just throw all the food scraps into the compost bin?
\item I thought everything organic would decompose eventually?
\end{enumerate}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\choiceitem{A}{c -- a -- d -- b}\hfill \choiceitem{B}{d -- a -- c -- b}\hfill \choiceitem{C}{d -- b -- c -- a}\hfill \choiceitem{D}{c -- b -- d -- a}}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{\alph*.}, leftmargin=2em, itemsep=3pt, topsep=2pt]
\item Not everything, but it shows you acknowledge the mistake.
\item Exactly, the action that follows is what truly matters.
\item But what if saying sorry isn’t enough to make things right?
\item So, you’re saying the apology is just the first step?
\item Do you really think an apology can fix everything?
\item It's not just the words, it’s the sincerity behind them and what you do after.
\end{enumerate}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{d -- b -- c -- f -- e -- a}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{d -- f -- e -- a -- c -- b}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{e -- a -- c -- f -- d -- b}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}
\begin{enumerate}[label=\textbf{\alph*.}, leftmargin=2em, itemsep=3pt, topsep=2pt]
\item There’s a parking garage a few blocks down.
\item I hope there’s space available; last time it was packed.
\item Let’s check it out before the concert starts.
\item Do you know if there are any parking spots nearby?
\end{enumerate}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\choiceitem{A}{b -- c -- d -- a}\hfill \choiceitem{B}{d -- c -- b -- a}\hfill \choiceitem{C}{d -- a -- b -- c}\hfill \choiceitem{D}{b -- a -- d -- c}}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\makebox[\linewidth][s]{\choiceitem{A}{c -- b -- a -- d}\hfill \choiceitem{B}{a -- d -- b -- c}\hfill \choiceitem{C}{c -- d -- a -- b}\hfill \choiceitem{D}{c -- a -- b -- d}}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}
\par\vspace{4pt}

\item Have you thought about composting as well?
\item I’ve been trying to adopt a greener lifestyle lately.
\item Yes, I’m looking into that! It seems beneficial for the garden.

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{d -- f -- e -- b -- a -- d}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{e -- d -- f -- b -- a -- c}\par\vspace{4pt}

\item It’s important for workers to upskill and adapt to new technologies.

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{a -- e -- c -- f -- d -- b}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{a -- b -- c -- e -- d -- f}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}

\question[Sắp xếp hội thoại]{Sắp xếp các câu sau theo đúng trình tự để tạo thành đoạn hội thoại hoàn chỉnh:}

\item That sounds good. What about exercise?
\item Don't forget about sleep and staying hydrated.
\item Regular exercise is key. Even just a 30-minute walk each day can make a big difference.
\item I've been thinking about making some changes to live a healthier lifestyle. Any tips?
\end{enumerate}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{f -- d -- c -- a -- b -- e}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{f -- e -- b -- d -- c -- a}\par\vspace{4pt}

\newpage
\begin{topicbox}{\large DẠNG 5: VIẾT LẠI CÂU}
\textit{Chọn phương án (A, B, C hoặc D) viết lại câu sao cho nghĩa không đổi.}
\end{topicbox}
\setcounter{qcounter}{0}
\begin{groupbox}\textbf{\large Nhóm viết lại câu đồng nghĩa}\end{groupbox}
\question[Viết lại câu]{While penicillin has been highly effective in treating bacterial infections, its overuse has led to the rise of antibiotic-resistant bacteria.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Although penicillin has been successful in combating bacterial infections, its excessive use has contributed to antibiotic resistance.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Despite penicillin's effectiveness against infections, its misuse has resulted in fewer bacteria responding to treatment.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The widespread use of penicillin has made it a powerful tool against infections, but over time, it has caused bacteria to become resistant.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Due to the fact that penicillin has treated infections well, its overuse has led to many bacteria becoming harder to control.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{It is generally accepted that climate changes are caused by human activity.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{It is accepted that human activity generally caused climate changes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Climate changes are generally believed to be caused by human activity.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{It is widely believed that human activity is responsible for causing climate change.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Human activity is generally accepted to be caused climate change.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I am sure he said something terrible to her because of her red eyes.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{He must have said something terrible to her because I could see it from her red eyes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{He could have said something terrible to her because I could see it from her red eyes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{He should have said something terrible to her because I could see it from her red eyes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{He may have said something terrible to her because I could see it from her red eyes.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Although I can’t be completely sure, it seems that approximately one hundred people will be attending the conference.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I can’t confirm the exact number, but I suppose one hundred people will attend the conference.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{It’s likely that around one hundred people will be at the conference, though I’m not certain.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I am certain that approximately one hundred people will be attending the conference.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{There are about one hundred people confirming to attend the conference, though I’m not entirely sure.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Because the documents had not been thoroughly reviewed by the committee, several errors were identified during the final inspection.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Since the committee failed to review the documents thoroughly, several errors were discovered during the final inspection.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Several errors were ignored by the committee during the final inspection because there was no thorough review of the documents.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The committee’s thorough review of the documents caused several errors to be unnoticed until the final inspection.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Despite the committee’s thorough review of the documents, many errors were found until the final inspection.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{It’s important for all residents to participate in recycling efforts every month.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{All residents are required to join the recycling efforts every month.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Participating in recycling efforts every month is essential for all residents.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{It’s crucial that every resident joins the recycling efforts monthly on purpose.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Almost residents must participate in recycling efforts monthly.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{She has such a kind and caring personality that everyone enjoys being around her.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{People are put off by her excessive kindness and care.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{She is too kind and caring for people to enjoy her company.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Her kindness and care make her a delightful person to be around.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{She is so kind and caring that people can’t stand spending time with her.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I was presented with three project proposals at work, none of which fit the company’s strategic objectives.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{All three project proposals I received at work aligned perfectly with the company’s strategic objectives.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I received three project proposals, all of which were suitable for the company’s strategic objectives.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The three project proposals I was presented with did not align with the company’s strategic objectives.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Although none of the project proposals I received fit the company’s strategic objectives, I considered them carefully.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{When the weather is particularly bad, it can be more efficient to take the train than to drive.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{In bad weather, driving is often the more efficient choice over taking the train.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Bad weather conditions sometimes make it faster to drive than to take the train.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Without severe weather, taking the train can sometimes be more effective than driving.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{When the weather is unfavorable, taking the train may be a more efficient option than driving.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{If the marketing strategy for our new service had been more effective, we would have attracted more clients.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{More clients would have been attracted to our new service thanks to the ineffective marketing strategy.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Our new service attracted fewer clients because the marketing strategy wasn’t effective enough.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The marketing strategy for our new service was so successful that it attracted many clients.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Since our marketing strategy was so good, we attracted many clients to our new service.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I believed he was the ideal candidate for the job, but it became clear that he lacked the necessary skills.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I was wrong to think he was the perfect candidate for the position, as he soon showed that he did not have the skills required for the job.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I initially thought he was well-suited for the job, but it quickly became evident that he lacked the necessary skills for success.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Although I believed he was the ideal choice for the position, I soon realized that he did not possess the essential skills.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{While I considered him a strong candidate, it became clear that he was proved the skills needed for the job.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I’m not entirely certain, but my best guess is that around fifty attendees will be present at the seminar.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{I can’t say for sure, but I predict about fifty people will attend the seminar.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{There must be around fifty attendees at the seminar, I guess.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I have no doubt that approximately fifty attendees will be present at the seminar.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Roughly fifty attendees have confirmed their presence at the seminar, but I’m unsure.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{While both smartphones have advanced features, Model X has a longer battery life than Model Y.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Model X has the longest battery life, although Model Y also has advanced features.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Model X and Model Y have equally advanced features, but Model X has a longer battery life.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Most smartphones have longer battery life than Model X, but Model Y has the most advanced features.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Model X has more advanced features than Model Y, but Model Y has a longer battery life.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{She didn’t fully recognize the significance of effective time management until she embarked on her career.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Not until she secured her first job, she realized how significant time management was.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{She failed to acknowledge the value of proper time management before having her first job.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{It was not until she advanced in her career did she graspe the essential role of time management.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Only when she commenced her professional journey did she appreciate the importance of managing time efficiently.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Because the meeting agenda had not been shared in advance, important topics were not discussed thoroughly.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The team forgot to discuss important topics because they did not receive the meeting agenda in advance.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Several important topics were not covered due to the lack of a shared meeting agenda.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Since the meeting agenda had not been shared beforehand, several important topics were overlooked.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Although the meeting agenda was provided, some topics were still not discussed thoroughly.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Because drinking too much coffee can cause increased anxiety and disrupt sleep, many people are advised to limit their caffeine intake.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Although coffee is popular, drinking too much can cause anxiety and sleep problems, prompting limits.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Drinking too much coffee can cause anxiety and sleep issues, leading many to reduce caffeine.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Excessive coffee consumption can increase anxiety and disrupt sleep, so people are advised to restrict their intake.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Since too much coffee causes anxiety and sleep disruption, it's recommended to cut back on caffeine.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{It is commonly recognized that homeschooling provides a more tailored education for children.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Homeschooling is commonly acknowledged that provides a more tailored education for children.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{It is recognized that homeschooling widely offers a more individualized education.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Homeschooling is widely seen as providing a more tailored education for children.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Children are widely recognized to be provided with a more personalized education by homeschooling.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Studying history more than anything else helps you understand the present better and reduces the likelihood of repeating past mistakes.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The more you study history, the less likely you are to repeat past mistakes and the better you understand the present.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The more you study history, the less you understand the present, and the more likely you are to repeat past mistakes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The more you ignore history, the less likely you are to understand the present and avoid repeating past mistakes.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The more you study history, the less relevant the present seems, and the better you understand past mistakes.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Right after the student handed in the project, he realized with a shock that he had completely missed the deadline.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The student realized he had missed the deadline before submitting the project, which shocked him completely.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{No sooner had the student handed in the project than he was shocked to realize he had missed the deadline.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Not until the student realized he had missed the deadline did he submit the project, feeling shocked.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Hardly had he been shocked about the deadline when he handed in the project.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{If they had left earlier, they would have caught the flight and avoided the delay.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{They missed the flight because of facing a delay when arriving too late.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{They caught the flight and avoided the delay after leaving early.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Leaving later than planned caused them to miss the flight and experience a delay.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{They arrived late, resulting from missing the flight and dealing with the delay.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{The more you read about history, the deeper your understanding of its impact on modern society will be.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Reading about history increases your understanding of its impact on modern society more than anything else.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Your understanding of history's impact on modern society becomes deeper when you read it more.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The more deeply you understand history’s impact on modern society, the more likely you are to appreciate it.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Understanding history's impact on modern society encourages you to read it more often.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{After spending several months in the new city, he gradually got used to the fast-paced lifestyle and the constant noise.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{He used to live with the fast-paced lifestyle and the constant noise of the new city after few months.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{He constantly got accustomed to the new city because of the fast-paced lifestyle and constant noise.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{With several months to adapt, he continued to struggle with the fast-paced lifestyle and constant noise in the new city.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{After several months in the new city, he step by step became familiar with the fast-paced lifestyle and the constant noise.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Students caught cheating on their exams may face disciplinary actions, including suspension or expulsion.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{If students are found cheating during their exams, they could face severe disciplinary actions like suspension or expulsion.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Cheating on an exam can result in penalties, which might include suspension or expulsion, for students who are caught.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Being caught cheating during an exam guarantees that students will not face any disciplinary measures.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Teachers and administrators may choose to either take action or overlook instances of cheating on exams.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Due to unexpected issues, our team’s scheduled outing to the beach has been postponed.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The trip to the beach that we had arranged is now delayed because something unplanned happened.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Something came up at the beach, so they postponed our outing.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{We are no longer going to the beach because there isn’t enough time in our schedule to expect the issues.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{We can’t visit the beach at the planned time because it has many problems to expect.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Using public transport reduces travel costs and helps decrease traffic congestion.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Public transportation lowers commuting expenses and contributes to reducing traffic jams.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Taking public transport is both economical and effective, though it alleviates traffic congestion.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The affordability of public transport makes it beneficial, which also lessens traffic on the roads.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The more you use public transport, the less you save on travel costs, and the more congested the roads become.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{She opted to prepare a meal at home rather than dine out, aiming to save money and eat healthier.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{She decided against dining out, proving that preparing food at home would be both economical and beneficial for her health.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{She chose to cook her own meal at home instead of going out to eat, as it was more affordable and nutritious.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{She preferred dining out rather than preparing her own meal, as it was both convenient and healthier.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{She found that cooking at home could be less costly and more nutritious, so she never went out for dinner.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{The novel was praised for its intricate plot and well-developed characters, but some critics found the ending to be somewhat unsatisfying.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Although the novel's plot was complex and its characters were well-drawn, a few critics felt that the ending was boring.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Despite having a complicated plot and realistic characters, the novel's ending left something to be desired for some critics.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The novel was admired for its intricate plot and believable characters; however, its ending was considered to be somewhat underrated by a few critics.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{While the novel's plot was intricate and its characters were well-developed, the ending failed to meet the expectations of some critics.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Her persistence in learning a new language filled her with a great sense of accomplishment.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The progress she made in mastering a new language was extremely fulfilling.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Her determination to learn a new language impressed everyone around her.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{She was filled with satisfaction as she continued her persistence in learning a new language.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{She felt proud of her process in learning a new language thanks to her persistent efforts.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Paying with cash may provide greater advantages than utilizing digital money transfers.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Digital money transfers are often less advantageous than cash payments.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Cash payments are considered more beneficial than digital transactions.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Relying on cash for payments is certainly superior to using digital transfers.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Cash transactions likely offer more benefits compared to electronic money transfers.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Once the clock struck 5 PM, the workers hurriedly packed their bags and left the office, eager to head home after a long day.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The workers quickly prepared to eagerly go home after the clock indicated 5 PM.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The workers exchanged stories about their day as they walked out of the office right at 5 PM.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{No sooner had the clock turned 5 PM than the workers quickly grabbed their belongings and dashed out of the office.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{As the clock struck 5 PM, the workers took their time getting ready to leave, looking forward to heading home.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{She could barely finish the project on time because it was so complicated.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The project was complicated enough for her to finish on time.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Hardly could she finish the project on time because of its complications.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{She found the project complicated, but she finished it before the deadline.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{She struggled with the project, yet managed to complete it ahead of time.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Doubtless the greatest glory in living lies not in never falling, but in rising every time we fall.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{We can certainly take pride in rising every time we fall, not in never falling.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{We must feel proud of our constant success, but not in our failure.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{You may have fallen several times, but it could have been better to rise then.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{You should sometimes fail in order to see the great glory in success.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Living in the countryside offers fresh air and a slower pace of life, away from the hustle and bustle of the city.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Living in the countryside gives the same busy lifestyle as the city but with fresh air and open spaces.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The countryside offers a peaceful atmosphere with fresh air and wide spaces, contrasting with the fast-paced city life.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The countryside is less peaceful and more crowded than city life, despite the fresh air and open spaces.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The city offers open spaces, but the countryside provides a slower pace and less crowded lifestyle.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I could not finish the project on time because I had not gathered all the necessary data.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The project was too difficult to complete on time due to missing data.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I would have finished the project on time if I had gathered all the necessary data.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{I failed to complete the project timely because I was missing essential data.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The data I gathered was sufficient to finish the project on time.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I received two internship offers after completing my degree, neither of which matched my career goals.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Both internship offers I received after completing my degree aligned perfectly with my career goals.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I received two internships that were both suitable for my career goals after I finished my degree.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The two internships offered to me after completing my degree did not align with my career goals.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{After completing my degree, I was offered two internships, both of which were ideal for my career goals.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{While conservation efforts have helped some endangered species recover, many others continue to face the threat of extinction.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Although some endangered species have seen population increases due to conservation efforts, many are still on the brink of extinction.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{While conservation programs have successfully saved numerous species, they have also resulted in the decline of certain populations.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Despite the success of some conservation initiatives, numerous endangered species are still in critical danger of extinction.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Due to the fact that efforts to protect endangered species have made a difference, many continue to struggle with the risk of extinction.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{The presentation was clear enough that all the attendees understood the main points.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The presentation was too confusing for the attendees to understand the main points.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The attendees understood the main points because the presentation was so clear that it left no room for confusion.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The presentation was so vague that all the attendees understood the main points.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Everyone found the presentation such a confusing experience that they missed the main points.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Home workouts may be more convenient for people than going to the gym.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Going to the gym is likely not as inconvenient as home workouts.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Going to the gym is possibly not as convenient as home workouts.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Going to the gym is probably more convenient than working out at home.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Going to the gym is always less convenient than home workouts.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Just as the traffic light turned red, the cars abruptly came to a stop to avoid a collision.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Hardly had the traffic light changed to red when the cars suddenly halted to prevent an accident.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Scarcely had the traffic light turned red before the cars finally stopped to avoid a crash.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{No sooner had the traffic light changed to red than the cars began to slow down to prevent a collision.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Not until the traffic light turned red did the cars quickly stop to avoid a crash.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{I think it’s necessary to tell Tim about it at once.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Tim may be told about it at once.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Tim should be told about it at once.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Tim must be told about it at once.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Tim might be told about it at once.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{It is essential that we meet him at the airport.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{He must be met at the airport.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{He might be met at the airport.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{He should be met at the airport.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{He may be met at the airport.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{It’s very likely that the company will accept his application.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The company needs accept his application.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The company might/ may accept his application.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The company must accept his application.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The company should accept his application.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{Every student is required to write an essay on the topic.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Every student might write an essay on the topic.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Every student must write an essay on the topic.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{They require every student write an essay on the topic.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Every student should write an essay on the topic.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{It isn’t necessary for us to get a visa for Singapore.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{We needn’t get a visa for Singapore.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{We mustn’t get a visa for Singapore.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{We mayn’t get a visa for Singapore.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{We shouldn’t get a visa for Singapore.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Viết lại câu]{The girl just said hello and she is Tom’s youngest sister.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{The girl who just said hello is Tom’s youngest sister.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The girl saying hello is Tom’s youngest sister.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The girl just said hello is Tom’s youngest sister.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The girl, who just said hello, is Tom’s youngest sister.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\setcounter{qcounter}{0}
\begin{groupbox}\textbf{\large Nhóm viết lại kết hợp câu}\end{groupbox}
\question[Kết hợp câu]{The young musician has extensive knowledge of music theory. She has studied under renowned teachers for years.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Before she studied under renowned teachers for years, the young musician had extensive knowledge of music theory.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Much as she has studied under renowned teachers for years, the young musician has extensive knowledge of music theory.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{Having studied under renowned teachers for years, the young musician has extensive knowledge of music theory.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{With extensive knowledge of music theory, the young musician has studied under renowned teachers for years.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{The rise of remote work has improved employees' flexibility, yet it has also blurred the boundaries between work and personal life.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Although remote work has increased flexibility for employees, it has made it harder to separate work from personal life.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Remote work offers employees more flexibility, but it can also lead to challenges in maintaining a healthy work-life balance.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{While remote work enhances flexibility, many employees struggle to keep their work and personal lives separate.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Despite the benefits of remote work, employees often find it effortless to manage their personal and professional boundaries.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{He failed to enter college for two consecutive years. He was finally able to attend Vietnam National University.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{He gained admission to college for two consecutive years before finally failing to attend FPT Arena University.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{After failing to enter college for two consecutive years, he ultimately succeeded in attending Vietnam National University.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{He received rejection for two years but eventually achieved his goal of attending Vietnam National University.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{He spent two consecutive years enrolling at Vietnam National University before failing to gain acceptance into college.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{The purpose of exercise is to improve physical health. However, its benefits are realized only when we maintain a consistent routine.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{Exercise aims to improve physical health, and its benefits are always apparent regardless of routine.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{The benefits of exercise are realized only when we engage in a consistent routine by improving physical health.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{To improve physical health, exercise must be practiced consistently; otherwise, its benefits will not be realized.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Whether we improve our physical health through exercise affects how consistently we maintain our routine.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{Tom achieved his fitness goals because he was disciplined in his workout routine. He did not have any special equipment.}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{It was his discipline in following a workout routine, not special equipment, that helped him achieve his fitness goals.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{We aren’t sure if we’ll meet. It depends on our schedules.}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{We can certainly have a meeting, which depends on our schedules.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{I can either attend the concert or stay home and watch a movie. I prefer the latter choice.}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I make an effort to attend the concert before try watching a movie at home.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I have decided to attend the concert instead of watching a movie at home.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{The scientist conducted groundbreaking research on renewable energy sources. This work helped her earn several prestigious awards in her field.}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{What helped her earn several prestigious awards in her field was her groundbreaking research on renewable energy sources.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{What inspired her to conduct groundbreaking research on renewable energy sources was the recognition she received for her research.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{It was clear that the groundbreaking research on renewable energy sources could allow her to earn several prestigious awards in her field.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{She finally achieved her dream of becoming a doctor. It took years of learning.}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{By learning for years, she finally made her dream come true.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{She dedicated her many years of learning to fulfilling her aspiration of becoming a doctor.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{The weather is bad. The flight’s bound to be delayed.}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{The flight could have probably been delayed because of bad weather.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{I need to decide whether this weekend or wait until next month to visit my grandparents. I prefer visiting them this weekend.}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{I choose to visit my grandparents this weekend over waiting until next month.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{I’d prefer visiting my grandparents this weekend instead of waiting until next month.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{It was obvious that the conference featured experts from various fields might permit them to share innovative ideas and network.}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Where innovative ideas and networking flourished was at the conference featuring experts from various fields.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{They successfully launched a tech startup. It took years of planning and research.}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Years of planning and research are the driving forces behind their success in tech startups.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{They did not waste their time choosing to plan and research in years to operate a tech startup.}\par\vspace{4pt}

\question[Kết hợp câu]{The museum has extended hours today. I think that you’ll be able to visit before it closes.}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\begin{topicbox}{\large DẠNG 6: ĐỤC LỖ}
\textit{Đọc đoạn văn và chọn phương án đúng (A, B, C hoặc D) để điền vào mỗi chỗ trống.}
