\setcounter{qcounter}{0}
\begin{groupbox}\textbf{\large Nhóm câu hỏi đục lỗ dài}\end{groupbox}
\begin{readingpassage}[]
Throughout history, scholars have recognised that memory plays a central role in cognitive development. It is therefore essential to understand which techniques can help us memorize more efficiently. One of the most effective ways \textbf{(1)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Below are two simple techniques to help you remember everything, even a shopping list of eight items.

The first method involves creating a vivid and unusual story. For example, picture a giant loaf of bread with coffee suddenly spraying out like a fountain. The coffee turns into white yogurt, \textbf{(2)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Olives run across the bridge while large eggs chase them. The olives hide behind a carton of orange juice. When you try to eat one, it becomes a terrible-tasting onion. \textbf{(3)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

The second technique is called the "memory palace". Visualize your home and mentally walk through each room. Place the items from your list in specific locations, such as bread on the doormat or coffee in front of the TV. \textbf{(4)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, you make the information easier to recall. Not only is this strategy useful for shopping lists \textbf{(5)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.
\end{readingpassage}

\question[Điền khuyết]{Chọn đáp án đúng cho chỗ trống (1):}

\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{A}{may be use imagination}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{is to use imagination}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{are imagining effectively}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{is to avoid using imagination}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{the river then flows and passes through a bridge made from steak}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{which flows into a river passing under a bridge made of steak}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{flowing into a river that passed under a bridge made from steak}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{These strange and colorful images help fix the items in your mind}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Such usual mental sequences immediately quell your curiosity}\par\vspace{4pt}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Although smell is often considered the least valued of the five senses, scientists now understand that it forms one of the most immediate links between the brain and the outside world. A scent can reach the area of the brain connected with emotions and memories in just two seconds, which partly explains why certain aromas can instantly trigger vivid recollections. Yet the precise mechanism behind this influence remains unclear, \textbf{(6)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

Nevertheless, smell also \textbf{(7)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Apparently, people respond more strongly to subtle chemical cues than they realise, and even first impressions can be shaped by scent. Many individuals tend to be attracted to those \textbf{(8)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Interestingly, people also smell of what they eat, which may further affect how they are perceived.

In consumer behaviour, the impact of scent has become a topic of substantial interest. In one well-known study, a majority of participants reported being more willing to buy a pair of trainers when the shop environment smelled pleasantly floral. \textbf{(9)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. The use of fragrance has gradually become a marketing tool across different industries. Today, many companies deliberately integrate scent into their branding strategies to create welcoming environments and encourage customers to stay longer. However, experts warn that scent-based persuasion has its limits. \textbf{(10)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.
\end{readingpassage}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{because many people still do not pay attention to their sense of smell}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{so researchers have stopped all experiments related to the topic}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{but most experts agree that smell affects emotions very strongly}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{works as a main tool for solving difficult mathematical problems}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{The study concluded that floral shops are the most effective retail spaces}\par\vspace{4pt}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Cycling is an eco-friendly activity that helps protect the environment, and many countries in Europe, including the UK, are encouraging it as they face problems like air pollution and heavy traffic. \textbf{(11)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Over the past year, however, the number of people cycling in London has risen by an impressive 45 percent. This sharp increase mainly comes from the expansion of dedicated bike lanes, rising environmental awareness, and the city's ongoing efforts to reduce congestion and improve air quality. \textbf{(12)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

What has driven this change? Much of the progress is thanks to the National Cycle Network project, \textbf{(13)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. The city has also invested heavily in improving cycling facilities, allocating significant resources to upgrading routes, enhancing safety features, and supporting a more cyclist-friendly urban environment. The introduction of secure bicycle parking on streets, at railway and underground stations, and in schools and workplaces \textbf{(14)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Today, it is estimated that nearly 75 percent of the population live within two miles of a cycling route. \textbf{(15)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, people are making more cycling journeys than ever before, which is helping transform London into a healthier and more sustainable city.
\end{readingpassage}

\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{Most of Britain's 20 million bikes saw regular use, showing limited cycling enthusiasm recently}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{C}{In the 2010s, the British weren't keen on cycling, with only 5 out of 20 million bicycles in use}\par\vspace{4pt}
\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Hardly any enthusiasm for cycling was evident in Britain recently, as regular use accounted for almost all bicycles nationwide}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{In terms of pollution levels, other cities across Europe now rank lower than London}\par\vspace{4pt}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Urbanization, among one of the most alarming issues worldwide, \textbf{(16)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. This shift is not merely a change of scenery; it reflects a profound shift in lifestyles, opportunities, and challenges. As more people flock to urban centers in search of better employment prospects and improved living conditions, cities around the world are experiencing rapid growth.

While urbanization can bring numerous benefits, such as economic development and cultural exchange, it also poses significant challenges that need to be addressed. One of the most pressing issues is the strain on infrastructure, \textbf{(17)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. As cities expand, the demand for housing, transportation, and public services frequently exceeds supply, leading to overcrowded living conditions and increased traffic congestion. Many major cities, which are grappling with inadequate public transportation systems, force residents to rely on cars, contributing to pollution and exacerbating greenhouse gas emissions.

Moreover, urbanization can exacerbate social inequalities. Low-income families have been pushed into informal settlements or slums, and access to basic services is limited. \textbf{(18)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

To navigate the challenges posed by urbanization, effective urban planning, and policy-making are essential. \textbf{(19)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Furthermore, engaging communities in the planning process can foster a sense of ownership and responsibility. Addressing these challenges head-on, \textbf{(20)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.
\end{readingpassage}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{on which a large number of migrants from rural areas move to some big cities}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{struggling to make the influx of residential scale not beyond control}\par\vspace{4pt}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{D}{Residents who live here must suffer from difficulties in marginalized groups due to poverty and social frustration}\par\vspace{4pt}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Some holidays are celebrated in honor of family members like mothers or fathers. In Viet Nam, June 28th is a day for the whole family, \textbf{(21)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Its aim is to honour and spread traditional family values, and provide an opportunity for family members to get together and also connect with their cultural roots. Viet Nam, as a nation, \textbf{(22)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} with a profound emphasis on the enduring values of family loyalty and harmony. In Vietnamese culture, the family is viewed as a unit, and children are expected to demonstrate the utmost respect to their parents. Established on June 28th, 2001 by the Deputy Prime Minister, \textbf{(23)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

Various cultural activities are organized in cities all across Vietnam. This year’s celebrations are themed ‘Peaceful Family, Happy Society’, and include sports, cooking and singing contests. The event will also feature educational seminars on family values and parenting skills, as well as workshops on financial planning and career development. Families can participate in fun games and activities such as scavenger hunts, obstacle courses, and relay races \textbf{(24)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. The highlight of the day will be a concert featuring popular local artists, where families can enjoy live music performances and spend quality time together. \textbf{(25)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Globalization has brought about incredible changes in nearly every aspect of human life. From economic ties to technological advancements, the world is more interconnected than ever before. However, this growing connection between countries has also caused arguments \textbf{(31)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

One of the most notable impacts of globalization on local cultures \textbf{(32)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Through media, the internet, and global trade, people around the world are exposed to a wide variety of cultural influences. \textbf{(33)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. For example, the global popularity of American fast food chains, Bollywood films, and K-pop music clearly indicate how cultures can break their borders and to a more diverse global cultural landscape.

However, the same forces \textbf{(34)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} can also threaten the uniqueness of local cultures. The global dominance of certain cultures, particularly Western culture, has raised concerns about cultural imperialism. As Western media, fashion, and lifestyle choices increase, \textbf{(35)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. This can result in the loss of cultural diversity as local practices are dropped in favor of more globally recognized ones.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Climate change is one of the most pressing issues facing our planet today. Rising temperatures are causing polar ice caps to melt and sea levels to rise. Many coastal cities are now at risk of flooding during extreme weather events. \textbf{(36)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. This has led to increased discussions about the need for sustainable practices.

Efforts to combat climate change have been implemented worldwide. Countries are working together to reduce carbon emissions, \textbf{(37)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} through international agreements like the Paris Accord. Governments are also investing in renewable energy sources, which can help lessen our reliance on fossil fuels.

In addition to these initiatives, individuals can also make a difference. Simple actions, such as reducing waste and conserving energy, can contribute to a healthier environment. \textbf{(38)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, we cannot achieve significant progress.

Climate change affects not only the environment but also human health and well-being. \textbf{(39)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Rising temperatures can lead to increased respiratory problems and heat-related illnesses. Therefore, it is essential to address these issues urgently to protect our planet and ourselves.

As awareness of climate change grows, more people are joining the movement for climate action. \textbf{(40)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, which means that collective action is needed to create a sustainable world for generations to come.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
In our modern throwaway society, products are often discarded before their actual end of life, which creates massive waste problems globally. Had manufacturers designed items to last longer, we would see less waste in our landfills today. The growing repair movement, which started in small communities, \textbf{(41)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. People are learning to fix their broken electronics; consequently, they save money and reduce environmental impact. \textbf{(42)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

Many companies resist this movement because planned obsolescence drives their profits; however, sustainable businesses are embracing circular economy principles. Creative upcycling, \textbf{(43)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, transforms unwanted objects into beautiful, functional pieces. Children should be taught repair skills in schools to nurture a generation that values resources. Community workshops provide tools and expertise for those who want to extend their possessions. Even governments are starting to recognize the importance of this shift, with some countries now implementing "right to repair" laws \textbf{(44)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

The true value of repairing lies not just in waste reduction but in challenging our relationship with consumption. By reimagining how we use products, we create a more sustainable future where items are treasured rather than trashed. In a world with limited resources, the repair revolution isn't just practical—it's necessary for our planet's survival. \textbf{(45)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Our choices today will determine whether future generations inherit a sustainable planet or a wasteland of discarded goods.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
James Watson and Francis Crick set out to solve the structure of DNA by tinkering with toy-like physical models. \textbf{(46)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, they deduced the structure of the double helix. This is an example of how scientific breakthroughs can emerge from something that looks remarkably like play, \textbf{(47)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}.

A growing body of evidence is showing that play is a vital activity. It is associated with the development of cognitive and emotional skills, such as critical thinking, problem-solving, and resilience. \textbf{(48)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}, and the main mechanism for them to deal with that unknown is to play, sense, and experiment with things around.

Evidence is also emerging that certain kinds of play can help children learn as well as formal instruction. Guided play involves a playful educational task where children have freedom of choice, \textbf{(49)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. Research indicates that guided play is as effective as conventional teaching for developing key skills such as literacy, numeracy, and thinking skills.

There are links, too, between play and creativity, a central pillar of the scientific endeavour. You're creating, you are experimenting, you are buzzing ideas together. There is an obvious parallel with playing imaginatively or playing with friends. \textbf{(50)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}}. It eventually won Watson and Crick a Nobel Prize and unlocked one of the greatest mysteries of biology.
\end{readingpassage}

\noindent\hangindent=2.2em\hangafter=1\choiceitem{B}{By following their curiosity and investigating different possibilities}\par\vspace{4pt}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
A part-time job is generally considered to be employment that is less than 40 hours of work per week. Some employers consider between 32 and 40 hours of work per week to be full-time, \textbf{(1)} part-time jobs are usually classified as anything less than 40 hours.

Working on a part-time schedule has many perks and provides the optimal flexibility needed by some individuals. For example, parents with young children, students, and people \textbf{(2)} have out-of-work responsibilities often find that part-time work is their only option. Others work in part-time jobs because they are unable to find full-time positions - and working part-time is better for them than not working at all.

In some situations, part-time employees can earn as much as full-time employees on an hourly basis, but most employers do not offer \textbf{(3)} such as health insurance, vacation time, and paid holidays to their part-time employees.

There are many pros and cons to working part-time and there are also several specific industries that are known to have \textbf{(4)} part-time positions available. You should \textbf{(5)} the factors before deciding if a part-time or a full-time position will work better for your specific situation.
\end{readingpassage}

Reading is good for us. In fact, there is a great \textbf{(6)} of evidence that reading for pleasure is more than just another leisure pursuit - it actually improves our mental and physical health. Reading extended texts such as novels or biographies, \textbf{(7)} requires intense concentration for a considerable period of time, helps to lengthen attention spans in children and improves their ability to think clearly. \textbf{(8)}, experts say it is essential to acquire the habit of reading extensively as a small child, while the brain is still developing.

Reading can undoubtedly be beneficial to our mental well-being. Reading not only helps combat feelings of loneliness, it also allows people to \textbf{(9)} down and forget their problems for a while. The concentration required during the act of reading seems to ease muscle tension and slow the heart rate. Researchers have found that just six minutes of reading can \textbf{(10)} stress levels by as much as two-thirds.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
It's the end of the school year and the exam period is approaching. Undoubtedly, exams \textbf{(11)} pressure on both students and parents alike. \textbf{(12)} parents would like to see their children succeeding academically, as they believe a good education gives them an added advantage in life.

They want to see their children settled in a rewarding job and be financially well-off. But is that what their children have planned for themselves? Apparently it is not. Most students either can't think that far ahead or have no intention of doing so. They would rather aim low so as not to be disappointed if they get low marks.

However, child psychologists, who have been consulted on the matter, stress that students should aim high, but at the same time have a variety of other options. \textbf{(13)}, students will not have to worry about grades and in the long term they will have better chances of succeeding. On the whole, there are many practical and invaluable things \textbf{(14)} parents can do for their children.

Apart from being people their children can turn to, parents can plan a reasonable schedule that both they and their children agree on. According to psychologists' \textbf{(15)}, this schedule should include no more than eight hours of studying, three proper meals a day and some exercise. Above all, prior to each exam, parents need to reassure their children that everything will be fine whatever the result.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Whether you use social media to connect with friends and loved ones, watch videos, or simply “kill time,” the popularity of this pastime has increased significantly over the last decade. This is especially the \textbf{(16)} in children and teenagers, as well as young to middle-aged adults. So, how does a seemingly harmless hobby turn into an “addiction”? Like \textbf{(17)} types of behavioral addictions, using social media can influence your brain in harmful ways. You may use social media compulsively and excessively. You can become so accustomed to scrolling through posts, images, and videos that it \textbf{(18)} with other areas of your life.

Some experts estimate up to 10 percent of people in the United States have social media addiction. \textbf{(19)}, due to how common social media use is in general, the number of those who have social media addiction may be higher. Not everyone \textbf{(20)} uses social media will develop an addiction. Since this activity is becoming more accessible to more people, though, more people may develop an addiction to social media at some point in their lives.
\end{readingpassage}

If you think you'd be interested in participating in our exchange programme, why not contact us to discuss your particular requirements? \begin{readingpassage}[]
A new chatbot has passed one million users in less than a week, the project behind it says. ChatGPT was publicly released on Wednesday by OpenAI, an artificial intelligence research firm whose founders included Elon Musk. ChatGPT is the latest in a series of Als which the firm refers to as GPTS, an acronym which \textbf{(26)} for Generative Pre-Trained Transformer.

To develop the system, an early version was fine-tuned through conversations with human trainers. The system also learned from access to Twitter data according to a tweet from Elon Musk, \textbf{(27)} is no longer part of OpenAl's board. The Twitter boss wrote that he had paused access "for now". The results have impressed \textbf{(28)} people who have tried out the chatbot. OpenAl chief executive Sam Altman revealed the level of interest in the artificial conversationalist in a tweet. The project says the chat format allows the Al to answer "follow-up questions, admit its mistakes, challenge incorrect premises and reject inappropriate requests".

A journalist for technology news site Mashable who tried out ChatGPT reported it is hard to provoke the model into saying offensive things. \textbf{(29)}, OpenAl warns that "ChatGPT sometimes writes plausible-sounding but incorrect or nonsensical answers". Training the model to be more cautious, says the firm, causes it to decline to answer questions that it can answer correctly. Briefly questioned by the BBC for this article, ChatGPT revealed itself to be a cautious interviewee \textbf{(30)} of expressing itself clearly and accurately in English.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Imagine you are buying an apple in a supermarket. Which do you choose, one with a small brown mark, or one without? Be honest - you'd go for the apple \textbf{(31)} looks perfect. Supermarkets do this too, but on a much larger scale when buying fruit and vegetables from farmers. And what \textbf{(32)} of the ones with marks on them? They are thrown away. So are the ones that are a funny shape or size.

\textbf{(33)} reason for waste is that people buy more food than they can eat and supermarkets do everything they can to encourage this, for example with offers like “Buy one, get one free'. Developed countries waste about 650 million tons of food each year and so do developing countries. \textbf{(34)}, the waste happens for very different reasons. As the world's population grows, this problem will only \textbf{(35)}, so we need to take action urgently.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
The word "grandparents' is descriptive of the unique dual parenting role that this generation assumes. It emphasizes the vital part they play in family life. With a \textbf{(36)} of old world experience behind them, and with the unique ability to metamorphose from advisers or mediators into listeners or friends, they can offer support and stability in an ever-changing world. The underlying sense of responsibility \textbf{(37)} goes with this is tremendous.

Grandparents perform a balancing act between the needs of their adult children and those of their grandchildren. This role is varied. It is imperial at times, muted at \textbf{(38)}. It goes underground whenever required, but it is solid and absolutely dependable. Grandparents often \textbf{(39)} the gap between parents and children. Rebellious, independent children who are trying to find their feet are almost always at loggerheads with their parents. The role of grandparents can be very important provided that they act as impartial judges and are able to convey this feeling to both parties. One important thing, which seems to be missing in the lives of children today, is a sense of family, values, beliefs and principles. This is where the grandparents step in. \textbf{(40)}, instilling beliefs and values is not as easy as it was fifty or sixty years ago.
\end{readingpassage}

\begin{readingpassage}[]
Keeping fit and healthy may seem difficult, but there are a few easy-to-follow guidelines. Firstly, a balanced diet is absolutely essential. This means selecting food that is \textbf{(41)} in salt and sugar. Experts recommend \textbf{(42)} the amount of fat in our diet, as too much can lead to heart problems. They also suggest increasing the amount of high fibre food we eat. This comes in the form of fresh fruit, vegetables, whole meal bread and pasta. As well as being packed \textbf{(43)} vitamins and minerals, they are delicious too.

Secondly, it’s important to fit exercise into your daily \textbf{(44)}. This can be done by simply walking as much as possible and climbing stairs instead of taking the lift. Exercise is necessary to maintain a healthy body, as well as increasing energy levels and making you feel generally fitter and happier. Finally, staying relaxed is important for good health.

Too much stress can lead to a variety of illnesses, from headaches to high blood pressure. Whenever possible, do things you enjoy and treat yourself occasionally. So the message is simple - enjoy yourself but learn to respect your body too. It’s all a \textbf{(45)} of getting the balance right.
\end{readingpassage}

Despite the wealth of information campaigns telling people about global warming and its causes, \textbf{(46)} people have yet to realize how severe the problem is. Coming climate changes could alter as much as one third of plant and animal habitats by the end of the 22nd century. These changes could in turn cause widespread extinctions among plant and animal species around the globe.

Coastal and island habitats are perhaps in the greatest danger \textbf{(47)} they face the combined threats of warming oceans and rising sea levels. As habitats change, many animals will come under intense pressure to find more suitable homes for themselves. Mass \textbf{(48)} of at least some animals are certainly to be expected, but the fact remains that many animals will simply not be able to move fast enough.

Such dire predictions may sound alarmist, but they are based on the rather moderate estimate that the amount of carbon dioxide in the atmosphere will double by 2100. Many scientists believe, however, \textbf{(49)} this figure is actually very conservative, and they claim that a tripling is far more realistic. If they are \textbf{(50)}, the effects on nature will be even more dramatic.

Those who possess high emotional intelligence know better than \textbf{(16)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} impulsively in stressful situations, as they have developed the skill of emotional regulation. They are aware that reacting without thinking often leads to unnecessary conflicts or misunderstandings.

Rather than suppressing their emotions, individuals with emotional intelligence know how to \textbf{(17)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} frustration or anxiety in a constructive way. This ability not only helps them stay calm under pressure but also encourages healthy communication with others.

\textbf{(18)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} allows individuals to recognize their feelings and those of others, which is critical in managing both personal and professional relationships. It also contributes to reducing conflicts and improving teamwork in various environments.

When it comes to professional settings, emotional intelligence can be a significant advantage. Those who lack this skill often face a \textbf{(19)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} in their ability to work well with others, making collaboration difficult. However, by improving this skill, individuals can better manage their emotions and work more effectively with their colleagues.

In conclusion, emotional intelligence demonstrates \textbf{(20)}~\underline{\hspace{1.3cm}} in achieving personal and professional success. It enables individuals to navigate complex social situations and build strong, supportive networks.
